# Sakuya Cheat Menu Pro - Block 4 Military Scripted GUIs

sakuya_main_04_01_mark_army_open = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_force_empty_notice
        trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_01.1 popup = yes }
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_01_mark_army_open_marked_country = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_force_empty_notice

        every_country = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
                any_military_formation = {
                    is_army = yes
                }
            }
            root = { set_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found }
            save_scope_as = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_country_scope
        }

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found
                exists = scope:sakuya_main_04_01_marked_country_scope
            }
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_use_marked_country
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_01.1 popup = yes }
        }
        else = {
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_force_empty_notice
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_01.1 popup = yes }
        }

        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_01_mark_navy_player_all = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        is_player = yes
        any_military_formation = { is_fleet = yes }
    }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_force_empty_notice
        trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_01.2 popup = yes }
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_01_mark_navy_marked_country_all = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        is_player = yes
        any_country = {
            has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
            any_military_formation = { is_fleet = yes }
        }
    }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_force_empty_notice

        every_country = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
                any_military_formation = { is_fleet = yes }
            }
            root = { set_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found }
            save_scope_as = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_country_scope
        }

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found
                exists = scope:sakuya_main_04_01_marked_country_scope
            }
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_use_marked_country
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_01.2 popup = yes }
        }
        else = {
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_force_empty_notice
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_01.2 popup = yes }
        }

        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_mark_country_found
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_01_mark_army_clear_all_enabled = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        is_player = yes
        OR = {
            any_country = {
                has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_army_list
            }
            any_country = {
                has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_01_mark_army_clear_all = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    is_valid = {
        OR = {
            any_country = {
                has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_army_list
            }
            any_country = {
                has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list
            }
        }
    }

    effect = {
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_army_list }
            clear_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_army_list
        }
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list }
            clear_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list
        }
        every_country = {
            every_military_formation = {
                if = {
                    limit = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy }
                    remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy
                }
                if = {
                    limit = { has_variable = cmp_military_target_fleet }
                    remove_variable = cmp_military_target_fleet
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_01_take_marked_navy_ships = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        is_player = yes
        any_country = {
            has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list
        }
    }

    effect = {
        save_scope_as = sakuya_b4_ship_taker_scope
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list }
            every_in_list = {
                variable = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list
                limit = {
                    is_fleet = yes
                    any_scope_ship = { is_in_battle = no }
                }
                every_scope_ship = {
                    limit = { is_in_battle = no }
                    set_ship_owner_multiple = scope:sakuya_b4_ship_taker_scope
                }
            }
        }
        # Victoria 3 1.13: finalize the temporary ownership-transfer fleet after a batch.
        clear_ownership_transfer_fleet = yes
        sakuya_b4_clear_marked_navy_lists_effect = yes
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_01_take_marked_navy_ships_to_marked_country = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        is_player = yes
        any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
        any_country = { has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list }
    }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_ship_taker_found
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            save_scope_as = sakuya_b4_ship_taker_scope
            root = { set_variable = sakuya_b4_ship_taker_found }
        }

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = sakuya_b4_ship_taker_found
                exists = scope:sakuya_b4_ship_taker_scope
            }
            every_country = {
                limit = { has_variable_list = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list }
                every_in_list = {
                    variable = sakuya_main_04_01_marked_navy_list
                    limit = {
                        is_fleet = yes
                        any_scope_ship = { is_in_battle = no }
                    }
                    every_scope_ship = {
                        limit = { is_in_battle = no }
                        set_ship_owner_multiple = scope:sakuya_b4_ship_taker_scope
                    }
                }
            }
            # Victoria 3 1.13: finalize the transfer fleet in the receiving country.
            scope:sakuya_b4_ship_taker_scope = { clear_ownership_transfer_fleet = yes }
            sakuya_b4_clear_marked_navy_lists_effect = yes
        }

        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_ship_taker_found
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_03_mark_political_movement_open = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_force_empty_notice
        trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_03.1 popup = yes }
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_03_mark_political_movement_open_marked_country = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        save_scope_as = sakuya_main_04_03_root_scope
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_mark_country_found
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_force_empty_notice

        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            scope:sakuya_main_04_03_root_scope = { set_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_mark_country_found }
            save_scope_as = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_country_scope
        }

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_mark_country_found
                exists = scope:sakuya_main_04_03_marked_country_scope
            }
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_use_marked_country
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_03.1 popup = yes }
        }
        else = {
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_force_empty_notice
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_03.1 popup = yes }
        }

        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_mark_country_found
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_03_mark_political_movement_clear_all_enabled = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        is_player = yes
        OR = {
            any_political_movement = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement }
            any_country = {
                any_political_movement = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_main_04_03_mark_political_movement_clear_all = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    is_valid = {
        OR = {
            any_political_movement = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement }
            any_country = {
                any_political_movement = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement }
            }
        }
    }

    effect = {
        every_political_movement = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement }
            remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
        }
        every_country = {
            limit = {
                any_political_movement = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement }
            }
            every_political_movement = {
                limit = { has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement }
                remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_pm_pop_attraction_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        OR = {
            any_political_movement = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_pop_attraction_active
            }
            any_country = {
                any_political_movement = {
                    has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                    has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_pop_attraction_active
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_pm_character_attraction_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        OR = {
            any_political_movement = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_character_attraction_active
            }
            any_country = {
                any_political_movement = {
                    has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                    has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_character_attraction_active
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_pm_radicalism_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        OR = {
            any_political_movement = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_radicalism_active

            any_country = {

                }

sakuya_b4_pm_enactment_impact_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        OR = {
            any_political_movement = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                OR = {
                    has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_radicalism_enactment_approval_active
                    has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_radicalism_enactment_disapproval_active
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                any_political_movement = {
                    has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                    OR = {
                        has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_radicalism_enactment_approval_active
                        has_variable = sakuya_b4_pm_radicalism_enactment_disapproval_active
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_pm_bolster_suppress_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        OR = {
            any_political_movement = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                OR = {
                    is_being_bolstered = yes
                    is_being_suppressed = yes
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                any_political_movement = {
                    has_variable = sakuya_main_04_03_marked_political_movement
                    OR = {
                        is_being_bolstered = yes
                        is_being_suppressed = yes
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_army_offense_add = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_army_offense_mult } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_army_offense_mult value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_army_offense_mult add = 0.2 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_army_offense
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_army_offense multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_army_offense_mult }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_army_offense_active
    }
}

sakuya_b4_army_offense_add_shift = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {

        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_navy_convoy_interception
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_navy_convoy_interception multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_navy_convoy_interception_mult }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_navy_convoy_interception_active
    }

        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_battle_combat_width_mult add = 0.2 }

        set_variable = sakuya_b4_battle_combat_width_active
    }
}

        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_mult } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_mult value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_mult add = -200 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_battle_casualties
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_battle_casualties multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_mult }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_active
    }
}

sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_add_alt = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_enemy_mult } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_enemy_mult value = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_enemy_mult add = 200 }

            add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_battle_casualties multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_battle_casualties_enemy_mult }

        }

# ============================================================
# 战争页 - 选择博弈/战争
# ============================================================

# 左键：打开博弈选择（玩家相关）
sakuya_b4_war_select_dp_open = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_force_empty_notice
        trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_02.1 popup = yes }
    }
}

# 右键：打开博弈选择（标记国家相关）
sakuya_b4_war_select_dp_open_marked = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        save_scope_as = sakuya_b4_dp_root_scope
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_mark_found
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_force_empty_notice

        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            scope:sakuya_b4_dp_root_scope = { set_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_mark_found }
            save_scope_as = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_marked_country_scope
        }

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_mark_found
                exists = scope:sakuya_main_04_02_dp_marked_country_scope
            }
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_use_marked_country
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_02.1 popup = yes }
        }
        else = {
            set_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_force_empty_notice
            trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_02.1 popup = yes }
        }

        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_dp_mark_found
    }
}

# 左键：打开战争选择（玩家相关）
sakuya_b4_war_select_war_open = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_force_show
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_war_use_marked_country
        remove_variable = sakuya_main_04_02_war_force_empty_notice
        trigger_event = { id = sakuya_main_04_02.2 popup = yes }
    }
}

# 取消选择 - 清空已选博弈和战争
sakuya_b4_war_clear_selection = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }

    effect = {
        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_selected_dp
        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_selected_war
    }
}

# 取消选择是否可用检查（用于enabled）
sakuya_b4_war_has_selection_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        OR = {
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_selected_dp
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_selected_war
        }
    }
}

# 战争借口（外交要求）- 选择/切换/检查
# ============================================================

sakuya_b4_wg_conquer_state_toggle = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_conquer_state }
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_conquer_state
        }
        else = {
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_return_state
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_take_treaty_port
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_unification
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_annex_country
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_make_protectorate
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_make_dominion
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_make_tributary
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_reduce_autonomy
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_transfer_subject
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_regime_change
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_ban_slavery
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_open_market
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_force_nationalization
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_humiliation
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_independence
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_liberate_country
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_liberate_subject
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_increase_autonomy
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_contain_threat
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_foreign_investment_rights
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_join_power_bloc
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_leave_power_bloc
            remove_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_restore_union
            set_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_conquer_state
        }
    }
}

            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_take_treaty_port
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_unification
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_annex_country
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_make_protectorate
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_make_dominion
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_make_tributary
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_reduce_autonomy
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_transfer_subject
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_regime_change
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_ban_slavery
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_open_market
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_force_nationalization
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_humiliation
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_independence
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_liberate_country
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_liberate_subject
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_increase_autonomy
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_contain_threat
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_foreign_investment_rights
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_join_power_bloc
            has_variable = sakuya_b4_wg_leave_power_bloc
        }
    }
}

# ============================================================
# 发起博弈 / 战争
# ============================================================

# 发起博弈 - 玩家 → 目标
sakuya_b4_start_dp_fwd = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_player
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_target
        }
        if = {
            limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_dp_target }
            sakuya_b4_create_dp_effect = yes
        }
    }
}

# 发起博弈 - 目标 → 玩家
sakuya_b4_start_dp_rev = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_player
        save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_target
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            sakuya_b4_create_dp_effect = yes
        }
    }
}

# 发起战争 - 玩家 → 目标
sakuya_b4_start_war_fwd = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_player
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_target
        }
        if = {
            limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_dp_target }
            sakuya_b4_create_war_effect = yes
        }
    }
}

# 发起战争 - 目标 → 玩家
sakuya_b4_start_war_rev = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_player
        save_scope_as = sakuya_dp_target
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            sakuya_b4_create_war_effect = yes
        }
    }
}

# ============================================================
# 激化度（Escalation）
# ============================================================

sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_active }
}

sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_add = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value add = 50 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_active
    }
}

sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_sub = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value add = -50 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers
        if = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value } add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value } set_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_active }
        if = { limit = { var:sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value = 0 } remove_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value remove_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_active }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_alt = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value add = 5 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_active
    }
}

sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_shift = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value add = 99999 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_active
    }
}

sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_clear = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_diplomatic_play_maneuvers
        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_value
        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_dp_maneuvers_active
    }
}

sakuya_b4_truce_remove_marked = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target } }
    effect = {
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            root = { end_truce = prev }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_truce_create_marked = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target } }
    effect = {
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            root = { create_bidirectional_truce = { country = prev months = 60 } }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_truce_create_marked_99_years = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target } }
    effect = {
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            root = { create_bidirectional_truce = { country = prev months = 1188 } }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = {
        OR = {
            has_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays
            any_country = {
                has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target
                has_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays
            }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays_add_marked = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target } }
    effect = {
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays }
        }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays_add_self = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays }
    }
}

sakuya_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays_clear = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
            remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_disallow_aggressive_plays
        }
    }
}



# === B4 HOME COUNTRY MODIFIERS BEGIN ===
sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_add = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult add = 0.5 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_active
    }
}
sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_add_shift = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult add = 999.99 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_active
    }
}
sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_add_alt = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult add = 0.05 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals
        add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult }
        set_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_active
    }
}
sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_sub = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult } } set_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult value = 0 } }
        change_variable = { name = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult add = -0.5 }
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals
        if = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult } add_modifier = { name = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals multiplier = var:sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult } set_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_active }
        if = { limit = { var:sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult = 0 } remove_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult remove_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_active }
    }
}
sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_clear = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { is_player = yes }
    effect = {
        remove_modifier = sakuya_cheat_b4_home_movement_radicals
        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_mult
        remove_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_active
    }
}
sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_check = {
    scope = country
    is_shown = { has_variable = sakuya_b4_home_movement_radicals_active }
}
